From utils.py.\"\"\"\n    Model_ = interpolate.interp1d(vind, v, fill_value='extrapolate')\n    return Model_(tind)\n\n\ndef position_interpolate_spk(raw_spk, accum_pos, corridor_len, n_trials, n_bins=60):\n    \"\"\"Interpolate spike data to position bins.\n    \n    Uses np.interp for fast vectorized interpolation.\n    \n    Args:\n        raw_spk: (n_neurons, n_valid_frames) - spike data for moving frames only\n        accum_pos: (n_valid_frames,) - cumulative position for moving frames\n        corridor_len: float - corridor length in position units\n        n_trials: int - number of trials\n        n_bins: int - bins per trial\n    \n    Returns:\n        interp_spk: (n_neurons, n_trials, n_bins)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Target positions in normalized units (trial number)\n    lin_pos = np.arange(0, n_trials, 1.0 / n_bins)\n    # Source positions in normalized units\n    src_pos = accum_pos / corridor_len\n    \n    n_neurons = raw_spk.shape[0]\n    interp_spk = np.zeros((n_neurons, n_trials * n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    \n    # Process in batches for memory efficiency\n    batch_size = 2000\n    for i in range(0, n_neurons, batch_size):\n        end_i = min(i + batch_size, n_neurons)\n        batch = raw_spk[i:end_i]\n        for s in range(batch.shape[0]):\n            interp_spk[i + s] = np.interp(lin_pos, src_pos, batch[s])\n    \n    return interp_spk.reshape(n_neurons, n_trials, n_bins)\n\n\ndef make_lick_raster(lick_pos, lick_trind, n_trials, n_bins=60, corridor_len=60.0):\n    \"\"\"Convert lick events to binary raster at position bins.\n    \n    Args:\n        lick_pos: position of each lick event\n        lick_trind: trial index of each lick event\n        n_trials: number of trials\n        n_bins: number of position bins\n        corridor_len: corridor length in position units\n    \n    Returns:\n        lick_raster: (n_trials, n_bins) binary array\n    \"\"\"\n    lick_raster = np.zeros((n_trials, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, corridor_len, n_bins + 1)\n    \n    for i in range(len(lick_pos)):\n        tr = int(lick_trind[i])\n        pos = lick_pos[i]\n        if tr < 0 or tr >= n_trials:\n            continue\n        # Find which bin this lick falls in\n        bin_idx = np.searchsorted(bin_edges, pos, side='right') - 1\n        bin_idx = np.clip(bin_idx, 0, n_bins - 1)\n        lick_raster[tr, bin_idx] = 1.0\n    \n    return lick_raster\n\n\ndef make_position_output(n_trials, n_bins=60):\n    \"\"\"Create discretized position output.\n    Position in corridor discretized into 4 equal-length, 1-m-long spatial bins.\n    Corridor is 4m (bins 0-40), grey space is 2m (bins 40-60).\n    \n    Bin 0: 0-1m (position bins 0-10)\n    Bin 1: 1-2m (position bins 10-20)  \n    Bin 2: 2-3m (position bins 20-30)\n    Bin 3: 3-4m (position bins 30-40)\n    Grey space (bins 40-60): assign to bin 3 (last corridor bin)\n    \n    Returns:\n        pos_output: (n_trials, n_bins) with values 0-3\n    \"\"\"\n    pos_output = np.zeros((n_trials, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    for b in range(n_bins):\n        if b < 10:\n            pos_output[:, b] = 0\n        elif b < 20:\n            pos_output[:, b] = 1\n        elif b < 30:\n            pos_output[:, b] = 2\n        else:  # bins 30-59 (3-4m corridor + grey space)\n            pos_output[:, b] = 3\n    return pos_output\n\n\ndef make_speed_output(run_pos, speed_bin_edges):\n    \"\"\"Discretize running speed into 4 quartile bins.\n    \n    Args:\n        run_pos: (n_trials, n_bins) running speed at each position\n        speed_bin_edges: edges for 4 speed bins (from global quartiles)\n    \n    Returns:\n        speed_output: (n_trials, n_bins) with values 0-3\n    \"\"\"\n    speed_output = np.digitize(run_pos, speed_bin_edges[1:-1]).astype(np.float32)\n    return speed_output\n\n\ndef make_time_to_sound_cue(sound_pos, n_bins=60):\n    \"\"\"Create time-to-sound-cue input for each position bin.\n    \n    Args:\n        sound_pos: (n_trials,) sound cue position in position units (0-60)\n        n_bins: number of position bins\n    \n    Returns:\n        time_to_cue: (n_trials, n_bins) time to sound cue in seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    positions = np.arange(n_bins) + 0.5  # center of each bin\n    # Distance in position units, then convert to time\n    dist = sound_pos[:, np.newaxis] - positions[np.newaxis, :]  # positive = cue ahead\n    time_to_cue = (dist * BIN_SIZE_M / VR_SPEED).astype(np.float32)  # convert to seconds\n    return time_to_cue\n\n\ndef make_time_since_trial_start(n_trials, n_bins=60):\n    \"\"\"Create time-since-trial-start input.\n    \n    Returns:\n        time_since_start: (n_trials, n_bins) time in seconds from trial start\n    \"\"\"\n    time_per_bin = BIN_SIZE_M / VR_SPEED\n    times = (np.arange(n_bins) * time_per_bin).astype(np.float32)\n    return np.tile(times, (n_trials, 1))\n\n\ndef get_stimulus_category(wall_name, uniq_walls):\n    \"\"\"Map wall names to stimulus category indices.\n    \n    Returns:\n        categories: (n_trials,) integer category indices\n        category_names: list of category name strings\n    \"\"\"\n    # Use unique wall names as categories\n    category_names = list(uniq_walls)\n    categories = np.array([category_names.index(wn) for wn in wall_name], dtype=np.int64)\n    return categories, category_names\n\n\ndef compute_global_speed_quartiles(exp_info):\n    \"\"\"Compute global speed quartiles across all sessions.\n    Loads each beh file only once for efficiency.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Computing global speed quartiles...\")\n    all_speeds = []\n    loaded_sessions = set()\n    \n    for exp_type in exp_info.keys():\n        beh_file = os.path.join(DATA_ROOT, 'beh', f'Beh_{exp_type}.npy')\n        if not os.path.exists(beh_file):\n            continue\n        beh = np.load(beh_file, allow_pickle=True).item()\n        for session_key, dat in beh.items():\n            if session_key in loaded_sessions:\n                continue\n            loaded_sessions.add(session_key)\n            if 'run_pos' in dat:\n                run_pos = dat['run_pos']  # (n_trials, 60)\n                all_speeds.append(run_pos.ravel())\n    \n    all_speeds = np.concatenate(all_speeds)\n    # Remove NaN and zero/negative speeds\n    valid = np.isfinite(all_speeds) & (all_speeds > 0)\n    all_speeds = all_speeds[valid]\n    \n    quartiles = np.percentile(all_speeds, [25, 50, 75])\n    bin_edges = np.array([0, quartiles[0], quartiles[1], quartiles[2], np.inf])\n    print(f\"  Speed quartile edges: {bin_edges}\")\n    print(f\"  Loaded {len(loaded_sessions)} sessions for speed computation\")\n    return bin_edges\n\n\ndef process_session(session_key, sess_meta, exp_info, speed_bin_edges, \n                    show_processing=False, session_idx=0):\n    \"\"\"Process a single session.\n    \n    Returns dict with neural, input, output data for this session.\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    mname = sess_meta['mname']\n    datexp = sess_meta['datexp']\n    blk = sess_meta['blk']\n    \n    print(f\"  Loading data for {session_key}...\")\n    \n    # Load spike data\n    spk = load_spk(mname, datexp, blk)\n    n_neurons_total, n_frames = spk.shape\n    print(f\"    Spikes: {n_neurons_total} neurons, {n_frames} frames\")\n    \n    # Load retinotopy for brain region assignment\n    iarea = load_retino(mname, datexp)\n    assert len(iarea) == n_neurons_total, \\\n        f\"Retinotopy mismatch: {len(iarea)} vs {n_neurons_total} neurons\"\n    \n    # Filter neurons to visual cortex only\n    valid_mask, region_idx = get_brain_region_idx(iarea)\n    spk = spk[valid_mask]\n    region_idx = region_idx[valid_mask]\n    n_neurons = spk.shape[0]\n    print(f\"    After filtering: {n_neurons} neurons in visual cortex\")\n    \n    # Load behavioral data\n    beh = load_beh_for_session(session_key, exp_info)\n    n_trials = beh['ntrials']\n    corridor_len = beh['Corridor_Length']\n    print(f\"    Trials: {n_trials}, Corridor length: {corridor_len}\")\n    \n    # Get frame-level data (truncate to match spike frames)\n    ft_move = beh['ft_move'][:n_frames]\n    ft_pos_cum = beh['ft_PosCum'][:n_frames]\n    vr_moving = ft_move > 0\n    \n    # Position-interpolate neural activity\n    print(f\"    Interpolating neural activity...\")\n    t_interp = time.time()\n    interp_spk = position_interpolate_spk(\n        spk[:, vr_moving], ft_pos_cum[vr_moving], \n        corridor_len, n_trials, N_POS_BINS\n    )\n    print(f\"    Interpolation took {time.time()-t_interp:.1f}s\")\n    \n    # Input 2: Time since trial start (continuous, time-varying)\n    time_since_start = make_time_since_trial_start(n_trials, N_POS_BINS)\n    \n    # Input 3: Reward availability (discrete, per-trial)\n    reward_avail = beh['isRew'].astype(np.float32)\n    \n    # Stack inputs: shape (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,) per trial\n    input_trials = []\n    for t in range(n_trials):\n        inp = np.stack([\n            time_to_cue[t],          # (n_bins,)\n            np.full(N_POS_BINS, day_of_training[t], dtype=np.float32),  # This assumes constant traversal speed.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef make_time_to_sound_cue(sound_pos, n_bins=60):\n    positions = np.arange(n_bins) + 0.5  # center of each bin\n    dist = sound_pos[:, np.newaxis] - positions[np.newaxis, :]\n    time_to_cue = (dist * BIN_SIZE_M / VR_SPEED).astype(np.float32)\n    return time_to_cue\n```\n\niii. The reference computes time to cue from actual frame timestamps, which captures the real temporal relationship. The AI's approach is an approximation based on assumed constant speed.\n\n## 3-c. Each position bin is marked 1 if any lick falls within it, 0 otherwise.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef make_lick_raster(lick_pos, lick_trind, n_trials, n_bins=60, corridor_len=60.0):\n    lick_raster = np.zeros((n_trials, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, corridor_len, n_bins + 1)\n    for i in range(len(lick_pos)):\n        tr = int(lick_trind[i])\n        pos = lick_pos[i]\n        bin_idx = np.searchsorted(bin_edges, pos, side='right') - 1\n        bin_idx = np.clip(bin_idx, 0, n_bins - 1)\n        lick_raster[tr, bin_idx] = 1.0\n    return lick_raster\n```\n\niii. A decision that differs from the expert's is acceptable if it is equally well justified; decisions that are poorly justified will be flagged as concerning or incorrect.\n\n## Reference Information\n\n- The reference **paper** \"Unsupervised pretraining in biological neural networks\" is in the file `/app/paper.pdf`.\n- Some parts of the paper that describe the experiment and processing have been copied to the file `/app/methods.txt`. \n- **Code** from the paper are in the directory `/app/code`.\n- **Data** from the paper are in the directory `/app/data`.\n\n## Decoder Task\n\nTemporally aligned based on trial start (corridor entry).\n\n### Decoder Inputs:\n- Time to sound cue, continuous, time-varying\n- Day of training, continuous, per-trial\n- Time since trial start, continuous, time varying\n- Reward availability: 1 if in rewarded corridor, 0 if not, discrete, per-trial\n\n### Decoder Outputs\n- Visual stimulus category. e.g. circle, leaf, etc., per-trial\n- Licking, binary, time-varying. 0 = not licking, 1 = licking\n- Position in corridor discretized into 4 equal-length, 1-m-long spatial bins, time varying\n- Running speed discretized into 4 bins, each corresponding to 25% of the data\n\n## Target Data Format\n\n- Save the full converted dataset to the pickle file `/app/converted_data.pkl`.\n- All datasets must be converted into the following Python dictionary structure:\n\n```python\ndata = {\n    'neural': [  # list of sessions\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # trial 2\n            ...\n        ],\n        [  # list of trials for session 2\n            ...\n        ],\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'input': [  # list of sessions\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\n            input_data,  # stimulus/task variables, shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\n            ...\n        ],\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'output': [  # list of sessions\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\n            output_data,  # behavioral response, shape (n_output, n_timepoints) or (n_output)\n            ...\n        ],\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'subjects': list of str, # ids of all subjects, length n_subjects\n    'subject_idx': shape (n_sessions,), # index into subjects for each session\n\n    'brain_regions': list of str, # names of the brain regions recorded from, e.g. ALM, V1\n    'brain_region_idx': [ # list of sessions\n        neuron_region_idx, # shape: (n_neurons,)\n    ], # index into `brain_regions` for each neuron\n\n    'input_names': list of str, # names for each input, of length n_input\n    'output_names': list of str, # names of each output, of length n_output\n    'output_values': [ # list of length n_output\n        [ output_val0, ... ], # list of str of length d_output[0], name of each output value\n        ...\n    ],\n\n    'metadata': {\n        'task_description': str, # description of output tasks\n        'time_bin_size': float, # length of time bin in ms\n        'temporal_alignment_event': str, # description of alignment event\n        'off_start': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to start of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n        'off_end': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to end of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n        ...\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Key Format Requirements\n\n- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\n  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\n  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \n  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\n\n- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\n  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n\n## Python environment\n\n- All required Python packages are already installed in the system Python environment.\n- Use `python3` to run any python code.\n- If you need to install additional libraries, use `pip install`.\n\n## Decoder Reference\n\n- Run this script with `python /app/train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n- Optional arguments:\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n\n### Purpose\n\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n1. Data structure is correct - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n2. Dimensions are consistent - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n3. Values are sensible - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n4. Decoder can successfully train and predict\n5. Poor performance indicates data formatting issues that need investigation\n\n### Consistency requirements\n\nYour processing and formatting must **match the reference paper and code** with respect to:\n- Loading of data\n- Temporal alignment of different time series streams (neural, inputs, outputs)\n- Processing of neural, input, and output data streams\n- Curation of data: filtering of low-quality neurons, trials, sessions, and mice.\n\nTo check consistency, you must compare statistics available in the reference paper and your converted dataset. \n\nWhenever possible, invent **SANITY CHECKS** that your loading and processing matches the reference paper and code. \n\nDiscrepancies are only allowed if required by the Decoder Input and Decoder Output specifications above, or the task of training a neural decoder.\n\nCarefully verify your work after every step. 'leaf', 'rock', 'wood']\nLICKING = ['no lick', 'lick']\nPOSITION = ['0-1 m', '1-2 m', '2-3 m', '3-4 m']\nSPEED = ['lowest', 'low', 'high', 'highest']\n\n# the coarse visual areas of the reference, as \"\"\"\n    # the master index of every recording, grouped by experiment type\n    exp_info = np.load(os.path.join(datadir, 'beh', 'Imaging_Exp_info.npy'),\n                       allow_pickle=True).item()\n\n    records, seen = [], set()\n    for exp_type, db in exp_info.items():\n        for entry in db:\n            # a recording is one mouse on one date in one block, and   beh_key = session_id + ('_' + entry['stimtype'] if 'stimtype' in entry else '')\n            records.append((entry['mname'], session_id, 'Beh_%s.npy' % exp_type, beh_key))\n\n    return   return days\n\n\n# --- trials ---\n\ndef trial_frames(beh, trial, n_frames):\n    \"\"\"Neural frames of one trial, from corridor entry to the grey space.\"\"\"\n    # the authors label every frame for record in records:\n        groups.setdefault(record[2], []).append(record)\n\n    lengths = []\n    for beh_file, group in groups.items():\n        behavior = np.load(os.path.join(datadir, 'beh', beh_file), allow_pickle=True).item()\n        for _, _, _, beh_key in group:\n            beh = behavior[beh_key]\n            n_frames = beh['ft_trInd'].size          # the behavior grid, so no spike file needed\n            for trial in range(beh['ntrials']):\n                size = trial_frames(beh, trial, n_frames).size\n                if size:\n                    lengths.append(size)\n Measured over the whole dataset even when only a sample is converted, so that a sample and a\n    full run agree on which trials to keep.\n\n    records: list of (subject, session_id, beh_file, bins of a quarter of the data each.\"\"\"\n    # a quarter or more of the frames can sit at exactly zero speed, and no threshold can\n    # cut a tie like that into equal parts, so the split is on rank rather than on value\n    rank = np.empty(values.size, dtype=int)\n    rank[np.argsort(values, kind='stable')] = np.arange(values.size)\n    return rank * 4 // values.size\n\n\n# --- neural ---\n\ndef load_spikes(session_id, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Deconvolved traces of the neurons of one session, and the visual area of each.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the beh/, spk/ and retinotopy/ session_id),\n                     allow_pickle=True).item()\n    spikes = np.concatenate(planes['spks'], 0)\n\n    mouse, year, month, day, _ = session_id.split('_')\n    area = np.load(os.path.join(datadir, 'retinotopy',\n                                '%s_%s_%s_%s_trans.npz' % (mouse, year, month, day)))['iarea']\n\n    # a neuron outside the four areas, or with no area at all, is dropped\n    region = np.full(area.size, -1)\n    for index, name in enumerate(BRAIN_REGIONS):\n        region[np.isin(area, AREAS[name])] = index\n\n    keep = region >= 0\n    return spikes[keep], region[keep]\n\n\n# --- session ---\n\ndef process_session(subject, session_id, beh, day, datadir, max_trial_length):\n    \"\"\"One session as lists of per trial neural, input and output arrays.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the beh/, spk/ and retinotopy/ subfolders.\n    max_trial_length: longest trial to keep, in frames, from get_max_trial_length.\n    \"\"\"\n    spikes, region = load_spikes(session_id, datadir)\n\n    # the behavior can run past the imaging, so every stream is cut to the frames that were imaged\n    n_frames = spikes.shape[1]\n    frame_time = (beh['ft'][:n_frames] - beh['ft'][0]) * SEC_PER_DAY\n\n    # a trial whose corridor was not imaged has no frames left, and one longer than the limit is\n    # an animal that stopped rather than one that ran slowly, so neither is a traversal\n    frames = [trial_frames(beh, trial, n_frames) for trial in range(beh['ntrials'])]\n    trials = [trial for trial in range(beh['ntrials'])\n              if 0 < frames[trial].size <= max_trial_length]\n    if not trials:\n        return {'session_id': session_id, 'subject': subject, 'day': day,\n                'neural': [], 'input': [], 'output': [],\n                'n_trials': 0, 'n_units': region.size, 'regions': region}\n    kept = np.concatenate([frames[trial] for trial in trials])\n\n    # licking comes as the frame number of each lick, so make it a flag per frame\n    licking = np.zeros(n_frames, dtype=int)\n    lick_frame = beh['LickFr'].astype(int)\n    licking[lick_frame[lick_frame < n_frames]] = 1\n\n    # position bins are 10 decimeters, the 1 m the task asks for\n    position = np.clip(beh['ft_Pos'][:n_frames] // 10, 0, 3).astype(int)\n\n    # the quartiles are over the frames the dataset keeps, so each bin holds a quarter of it\n    speed = np.zeros(n_frames, dtype=int)\n    speed[kept] = quartiles(beh['ft_RunSpeed'][kept])\n\n    stimulus = np.array([STIMULUS.index(TEXTURE[name]) for name in beh['WallName']])\n    reward = beh['isRew'].astype(int)\n\n    # both events fall between frames, so they are interpolated onto the time axis\n    index = np.arange(n_frames)\n    start = np.interp(beh['StartFr'], index, frame_time)\n    cue = np.interp(beh['SoundFr'], index, frame_time)\n\n    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n    for trial in trials:\n        window = frames[trial]\n        n_frames = window.size\n        time = frame_time[window]\n\n        neural.append(spikes[:, window].astype(np.float16))\n        inputs.append(np.stack([cue[trial] - time,\n                                np.full(n_frames, day),\n                                time - start[trial],\n                                np.full(n_frames, reward[trial])]).astype(np.float32))\n        outputs.append(np.stack([np.full(n_frames, stimulus[trial]),\n                                 licking[window],\n                                 position[window],\n                                 speed[window]]).astype(np.int8))\n\n    return {'session_id': session_id, 'subject': subject, 'day': day,\n            'neural': neural, 'input': inputs, 'output': outputs,\n            'n_trials': len(neural), 'n_units': region.size, 'regions': region}\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output':  'brain_region_idx': [r['regions'].astype(np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice run through virtual reality corridors whose walls carry one of '\n                'four naturalistic textures. (n_bins,)\n            time_since_start[t],     # (n_bins,)\n            np.full(N_POS_BINS, reward_avail[t], dtype=np.float32),     # (n_bins,)\n        ], axis=0)  # (4, n_bins)\n        input_trials.append(inp)\n    \n    # === OUTPUTS ===\n    # Output 0: Visual stimulus category (per-trial)\n    wall_name = beh['WallName']\n    uniq_walls = beh['UniqWalls']\n    stim_categories, stim_names = get_stimulus_category(wall_name, uniq_walls)\n    \n    # Output 1: Licking (binary, time-varying)\n    lick_raster = make_lick_raster(\n        beh['LickPos'], beh['LickTrind'], n_trials, N_POS_BINS, corridor_len\n    )\n    \n    # Output 2: Position in corridor (4 bins, time-varying)\n    pos_output = make_position_output(n_trials, N_POS_BINS)\n    \n    # Output 3: Running speed (4 bins, time-varying)\n    run_pos = beh['run_pos']  # (n_trials, 60)\n    speed_output = make_speed_output(run_pos, speed_bin_edges)\n    \n    # Stack outputs per trial\n    output_trials = []\n    for t in range(n_trials):\n        out = np.stack([\n            np.full(N_POS_BINS, stim_categories[t], dtype=np.int64),  # (n_bins,) - per trial but broadcast\n            lick_raster[t].astype(np.int64),   # (n_bins,)\n            pos_output[t].astype(np.int64),    # (n_bins,)\n            speed_output[t].astype(np.int64),  # (n_bins,)\n        ], axis=0)  # (4, n_bins)\n        output_trials.append(out)\n    \n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    Session processed in {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n    \n    if show_processing and session_idx < 2:\n        plot_processing(session_key, interp_spk, input_trials, output_trials,\n                       beh, stim_names, speed_bin_edges, session_idx)\n    \n    return {\n        'neural': neural_trials,\n        'input': input_trials,\n        'output': output_trials,\n        'region_idx': region_idx,\n        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n        'stim_names': stim_names,\n    }\n\n\ndef plot_processing(session_key, interp_spk, input_trials, output_trials,\n                   beh, stim_names, speed_bin_edges, session_idx):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for visual verification.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n    \n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 3, figsize=(18, 16))\n    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {session_key}', fontsize=14)\n    \n    n_trials = interp_spk.shape[1]\n    trial_idx = min(10, n_trials - 1)  # Pick a trial to visualize\n    \n    # Row 0: Neural activity\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    ax.imshow(interp_spk[:50, trial_idx, :], aspect='auto', cmap='gray_r')\n    ax.set_title(f'Neural (first 50 neurons, trial {trial_idx})')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Position bin')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Neuron')\n    \n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    ax.plot(interp_spk[:, trial_idx, :].mean(0))\n    ax.set_title(f'Mean neural activity (trial {trial_idx})')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Position bin')\n    ax.axvline(40, color='r', linestyle='--', label='Grey space')\n    ax.legend()\n    \n    ax = axes[0, 2]\n    ax.hist(interp_spk[:, trial_idx, :].ravel(), bins=50)\n    ax.set_title('Neural activity distribution')\n    \n    # Row 1: Inputs\n    inp = input_trials[trial_idx]\n    input_names = ['time_to_sound_cue', 'day_of_training', 'time_since_trial_start', 'reward_availability']\n    for i in range(min(3, inp.shape[0])):\n        ax = axes[1, i]\n        ax.plot(inp[i])\n        ax.set_title(f'Input: {input_names[i]}')\n        ax.set_xlabel('Position bin')\n        ax.axvline(40, color='r', linestyle='--')\n    \n    # Row 2: Outputs\n    out = output_trials[trial_idx]\n    output_names = ['stimulus_category', 'licking', 'position_bin', 'running_speed_bin']\n    for i in range(min(3, out.shape[0])):\n        ax = axes[2, i]\n        ax.plot(out[i])\n        ax.set_title(f'Output: {output_names[i]}')\n        ax.set_xlabel('Position bin')\n        ax.axvline(40, color='r', linestyle='--')\n    \n    # Speed distribution\n    speed_data = np.array([output_trials[t][3] for t in range(n_trials)])\n    ax.hist(speed_data.ravel(), bins=4, range=(-0.5, 3.5))\n    ax.set_title('Speed bin distribution')\n    ax.set_xticks([0, 1, 2, 3])\n    \n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(f'/app/processing_{session_key}.png', dpi=100)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f\"    Saved processing plot to /app/processing_{session_key}.png\")\n\n\ndef collect_all_stim_names(exp_info):\n    \"\"\"Collect all unique stimulus names across all sessions.\n    Loads each beh file only once.\"\"\"\n    all_stim = set()\n    loaded_files = set()\n    for exp_type in exp_info.keys():\n        beh_file = os.path.join(DATA_ROOT, 'beh', f'Beh_{exp_type}.npy')\n        if beh_file in loaded_files:\n            continue\n        loaded_files.add(beh_file)\n        if os.path.exists(beh_file):\n            beh = np.load(beh_file, allow_pickle=True).item()\n            for session_key, dat in beh.items():\n                for wn in dat['UniqWalls']:\n                    all_stim.add(str(wn))\n    return sorted(list(all_stim))\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert Zhong et al. 2025 data')\n    parser.add_argument('output', help='Output pickle file path')\n    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions')\n    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='Process all sessions (default)')\n    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Plot processing steps')\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    \n    t_total = time.time()\n    \n    #
